PiG -> RCNL2025 markers exported by this script:
  R_Shoulder             <= RSHO              | Right shoulder physical marker.
  L_Shoulder             <= LSHO              | Left shoulder physical marker.
  C7                     <= C7                | C7 physical marker.
  J_Notch                <= CLAV              | PiG CLAV marker corresponds approximately to jugular notch/clavicle marker.
  R_Elbow                <= RELB              | Right elbow physical marker.
  L_Elbow                <= LELB              | Left elbow physical marker.
  R_Wrist                <= RWRA + RWRB       | Average of the two right wrist bar markers.
  L_Wrist                <= LWRA + LWRB       | Average of the two left wrist bar markers.
  R_Asis                 <= RASI              | Right ASIS physical marker.
  L_Asis                 <= LASI              | Left ASIS physical marker.
  R_Psis                 <= RPSI              | Right PSIS physical marker, exported only when present.
  L_Psis                 <= LPSI              | Left PSIS physical marker, exported only when present.
  Sacral                 <= SACR + LPSI + RPSI  | Sacral marker from SACR when available; otherwise midpoint of LPSI and RPSI.
  Thoracic               <= T10               | PiG T10 marker used as thoracic posterior trunk marker.
  Sternum                <= STRN              | Sternum physical marker.
  R_Thigh_Lateral        <= RTHI              | Right lateral thigh tracking marker.
  R_Knee_Lateral         <= RKNE              | Right lateral knee marker.
  R_Shank_Lateral        <= RTIB              | Right lateral shank/tibia tracking marker.
  R_Ankle_Lateral        <= RANK              | Right lateral ankle marker.
  R_Heel                 <= RHEE              | Right heel marker.
  R_Toe                  <= RTOE              | Right toe marker.
  L_Thigh_Lateral        <= LTHI              | Left lateral thigh tracking marker.
  L_Knee_Lateral         <= LKNE              | Left lateral knee marker.
  L_Shank_Lateral        <= LTIB              | Left lateral shank/tibia tracking marker.
  L_Ankle_Lateral        <= LANK              | Left lateral ankle marker.
  L_Heel                 <= LHEE              | Left heel marker.
  L_Toe                  <= LTOE              | Left toe marker.
